# Things Josiah has already declined, dropped or ruled out

Do not offer these again. Each line names the memory note (in `memory/`) with his exact words and date.
An automatic appendix below lists every memory line with a decline/drop/never wording, so nothing is missed.

## Never (standing)
- Saving or backing up anything to OneDrive. Use `C:\Dev\Zomboidcraft-Desktop\additions`. (zomboid-pc-storage-rule)
- Fetching photos/Wikimedia from the cloud; refs only via his PC. (zomboid-no-wikimedia-rule)
- Vanilla Minecraft items or chests, anywhere. Replaced lights = our own light. (zomboid-no-vanilla-rule)
- Minecraft zombie or player models. The MC player model never drives the survivor body. (zomboid-survivor-lead-rule)
- Any Minecraft effect/art/animation in Endless Night (melee sounds, red hurt flash, damage tilt...). (endless-night-no-minecraft-effects)
- Franchise models/files in git, the jar or CI. (ASSETS-METHOD.md)
- Quick flat-shaded preview renders shown to him. Full detail only. (full-detail-previews-rule)
- Building anything before he approves its preview/mock-up. (zomboid-approve-mockups-first, zomboid-video-preview-rule)
- Threads resolving themselves without his OK. (zomboid-no-self-resolve-rule, zomboid-thread-resolve-rule)
- Hordes that vanish when you leave and return; vanilla despawn off; skip ServerCore (mob sleeping breaks this). (zomboid-hordes-never-vanish-rule)
- Flattening Reach levels; invisible walls; plain vertex-colour land or white patches. (zomboid-reach-terrain-rule, zomboid-reach-whole-world-quality-rule, zomboid-reach-layout-question)
- Distant Horizons or any far-render mod in the Reach world (Reach draws its own horizon). (endless-night-new-repo)
- Indian accents for any AI voice sample. (endless-night-voice-accents)
- Cloning or soundalikes of real voices (Jen Taylor / Cortana, Noble actors); Alloy/Nova/Sky voices never offered. (endless-night-cortana-ingame, zomboid-halo-voices)
- Nude body textures; female bodies always keep the underwear base set. (zomboid-pz-install-extract)
- Deleting the old ZOMBOIDCRAFT repo or county world. (MEMORY.md)

### photon-shader-settings.md (SHARED)
- Second round 2026-10-25 (he sent every Photon screen; fog visibly hazier after the first round): Colored Lights on, cloud shadows 1.0, sunlight 0.85, skylight 0.85, moonlight 0.6, auto exposure on with bias -0.3, saturation 0.80, contrast 1.10, aerial perspective boost 2, noctilucent clouds off, more cumulus coverage, aurora never, rain opacity 0.5, bloom 0.7. Shadows (2048, 8 chunks), GTAO, water and TAA+FXAA left as they were.
- 2026-10-25 (landscape thread) town FPS: 60 outside towns and 18 inside. Car detail levels raised it to 32; with shaders off it's 60 in town (probably the frame cap). So the rest is shader load from dense town geometry and lights. Iris's Max Shadow Distance slider doesn't show for Photon, because Photon sets its own. Suggested: Photon Shadows 8 → 6 chunks, Photon Colored Lights off, Photonics Max Lights 1000 → 250. CORRECTION (11:40): Colored Lights off turned his purple froglights amber (all light took the amber blocklight colour). Told him to turn it back ON; never suggest turning it off agai ...
- 2026-10-26: Josiah has NO Mod Menu. Particle Rain settings open with the /particlerain chat command. A "hide vanilla rain" toggle is unconfirmed. Photon Rain Opacity is in Shader Pack Settings (he had 0.5; suggested 0.25).
- LESSON 2026-10-26 (weather thread): Photon discards particle pixels below roughly 0.1 final alpha (texture x vertex alpha). Soft low-alpha mist drew as hard discs, then vanished entirely when thinned. Fix: particle textures with no faint pixels (solid body + rim of scattered specks that TAA smooths), and fade by size instead of alpha. The weapons thread found the same for beacon-beam glow (drops under ~half strength).
- 2026-11-01 (FPS thread): changing Photon's Profile resets its quality options (shadows, AO, clouds); his look values (fog, colours, exposure) survived in shaderpacks/photon_v1.4.zip.txt. Still upsetting to him (it happened after I suggested Medium as a test). Never suggest changing the Profile; test single settings instead. Restore sheet: /mnt/project-files/zomboid-mod/perf/photon-custom-settings.md (Profile High, then the custom list). His latest shadows: 1536 / 6 chunks.

Her Finger1 pivot = MIDDLE knuckle (PIP), so scale her by Tarkov wrist->PIP / hers (~0.84); wrists aligned, hand frame from wrist->knuckles + index->pinky; elbow 2-bone IK toward Tarkov elbow; fingers laid on Tarkov's D1-D2-D3 curve from the real knuckle line (palm verts beyond it blend in). Thumb rigid-aimed. Preview tarkov/previews/surv ...

Use idle_aim layer 3, walk_aim_0, Run_Stand_Slow_0 (skip Kaban_* boss clips). Head bone axes: local X=up, Y=forward -> kit/head rotation basis (Z,X,Y). Fix 89b8ab9a7, in playtest a6ca193: once aim is at least 0.92, the looked-through sight and anything on it is skipped (bench part via TarkovRig.draw skip, kit scope via kitBones). If neither can be identified, the whole gun is hidden. Lens only shows at aim of 0.92 or more.
- - 2026-11-08 10:30: with Photon, scope style A (crop) showed a BLACK lens and the gun hidden (lens copy empty; premultiplied blit over black disc, 429f662). - **How to apply:** every future Tarkov gun needs both loud and quiet shot audio when it can take a can; never spawn kit cans on found guns.

### zomboid-air-surfaces-tech.md (CORE)
- - tools/cars/surfaces.py [SRC] writes assets/zomboidcraft/cars/<id>.surfaces.json: body solid-triangle count + per surface kind, side (+1 = +x = left), pivot, unit axis (positive turn = trailing edge down / rudder TE left / LEF leading edge down), runs (start,count) into the opaque body mesh. F-22 parts come from the bought OBJ's object names (Elevator, Flap, Aileron, Rudder, Slat, Engine01 lower / Engine02 upper nozzle flaps) matched by centroid key, so SRC is needed for corvid. Su-27 parts are loose pieces of the decimated zcar inside boxes (stab, flaperon, rudder); it has no separate LEF or ...
- - AirSurfaces.java: control laws per jet, 75 deg/s actuators, no hydraulics below rpm 0.35. Own jet uses AirControls.command (stick), others use the body rates from qO->q. Applied through CarRenderer's per-triangle Turn loop at lod 0 only; running aircraft use 3x LOD distances so the surfaces show.
- - F-22 "no HUD" was never reproduced offline (it renders in the preview). Fix shipped: HUD drawn blended (entityTranslucentEmissive) on a 30% dark coating so it reads in daylight. CockpitBezels.java draws sharp MFD bezels with OSBs and the ICP keypad (face at z 5.75-5.77 under the HUD), legends lit in keys colour, and covers the glow keys under them.

### zomboid-attachment-colours-tech.md (CORE)
- - Java: GunState.COLOUR (String component zomboidcraft:colour). On a part it is the part's colour (it survives mounting: Attachments stores full stacks); on a gun it is its flip irons' colour. Attachments.COLOURS / IRONS_COLOURS / FLIP_IRONS (assault_rifle, hunting_rifle, precision_rifle, heavy_sniper; crossbow has NO irons since 2026-10-30; keep in step with guns.json "flip"), paint(stack, random) picks plain or a colour evenly. Called in Rolls MODIFY_DROPS loop and CaseItem.gunCase. NOT in the Carry tick (would re-roll plain stacks forever). AttachmentItem tooltip shows the colour.
- - skin.py only (no kit.py change, no weapons.py regen needed): attachment() and irons() wrap their specials in a custom_model_data select on those indices (coloured()); swap() collapses selects with index >= 10 back to the block model so reruns are idempotent; apply_items wraps loose items in a minecraft:component select on zomboidcraft:colour. `python3 tools/meshguns/skin.py` reapplies in 3 s (then revert rounding-only *_magazine.json). Gun item JSONs roughly doubled (assault_rifle 324K -> 692K).

### zomboid-chest-hole.md (SHARED)
- **Client:** ChestHole.of(mesh) cuts a holed copy once per outfit mesh (also the _low baked one), cached on ZBodyMesh.holed. It cuts every SPINE-bone quad inside 3 overlapping rects (spine space x/y, z -2.62..4.0 so backpacks/air tanks at z<-2.62 stay). Axis-aligned quads are split in texture space; slanted ones are dropped if their middle is in the hole. ChestHole.draw adds the flesh lining (grid outline walls, depth from what's worn over the core), and at lod<2 ribs, spine stubs, rim lips (z from surface()), and shreds (Gore.box). Gibs.chest throws kit/skin rags + gore out of the back.
- **Checks:** jtest HoleTest (scratchpad, backface-culled Java2D render, sky background shows see-through). Preview v21 (patch_chesthole.py, "Shotgun, point-blank" button). JS polygon cut in hole_logic.cjs/hole_run.cjs (stand-in THREE). Sources are in /mnt/project-files/zomboid-mod/characters/preview-src/.

- - The body is the mockup-1 "player" body: slim, tapered torso, separate thigh/shin/foot and upper/fore/hand, shoulders flush with the torso top, no neck, no face blocks, no thumbs.
- **Josiah 2026-10-26 00:02:** no black lines where garment pieces join (e.g. sleeve to forearm, thigh to shin, chest to waist). Clothing must flow as one piece; thin outline only on outer edges, cuffs, attachments. Implemented via gear() `join: 't'/'b'` (suppresses edge line, stitching and grime band at that edge) and one-box shoulder-to-elbow sleeves.
- **Josiah 2026-10-26 00:10:** no 3D hair (removed; heads use the skin). Kits must be roughed up to match the aesthetic: dirty, torn, bloody, zombie damage; clean 3D items looked out of place. Done in gear(): wear wash, mud, stains, holes cut through fabric, claw rakes, ragged hems, heavier blood; metal gets scratches and rust; hanging torn strips (tatters) and ripped-off sleeves. Wear scales with damage (KIT.wear 0.4/0.7/1.0).
- **Josiah 2026-10-26 01:01:** "still too clean and bright, doesn't match the moody aesthetic". Added grade(): every colour desaturated ~50-60% and darkened toward sooty brown #1f1b15 (hi-vis orange ends ~#7a553a), highlights cut to a third, skin tinted darker, preview lit overcast-cold + dim sodium lamp on dark wet asphalt. Default for all character art: muted, dark, never bright.
- **01:24:** Preview now runs the in-game walker anims (scratchpad zanim.js). Josiah: shamble looked like a moonwalk; fixed knee phase (knee bends while leg swings forward: shin = max(0, ±sin(step)), body lowest with legs spread). He approved ("much better"). The shipped WalkerModel still has the old cos(step) knee timing; offered to fix in playtest, no answer yet.

### zomboid-corpses-tech.md (SHARED)
- - The tickDeath mixin calls Corpses.holds: it holds the dead walker HOLD=36 ticks, lays the Corpse at tick 34 and removes the zombie at 36 with KILLED. Event 60 is never sent, so there's no puff.
- - zombie/Corpse: an entity that stores outfit, cut bits, look, the death long, variant and size. It can't be hit, pushed or picked, it falls if the ground goes, and it saves with the world. FROM (the zombie's id) hides it on clients until the zombie is gone. zombie/Corpses keeps a count per level; corpses.maxBodies (default 300) trims the oldest by laidAt. `Corpses.clear(level)` exists, but no command uses it yet.

### zomboid-gun-art-style.md (SHARED)
- **RULE (Josiah 2026-10-26 00:40):** "I want all guns and attachments made with this same design aesthetic (even wood grain or differing coloured ones). I prefer the black mag." **01:56 he reversed wear-by-condition: "I'd actually prefer all the weapons in their like new form at all times, inclusive of their attachments."** Approved concept: https://claude.ai/artifact/5QwwMvHvoMPKSSn7syQLCf. Every new gun, attachment, finish and camo must go through the same painter. Follows [[zomboid-clothing-style]]: no lines where a part's blocks join, thin outline only round each part's outside and each att ...
- - tools/weapons/toon.py, called from kit.paint() for every face (guns, part icons, ammo). Pieces = touching boxes of one material family (union-find, FAMILY folds 'mark' into 'anod' etc). cover() probes past each edge (DEPTH 0.03 sideways, DEPTH and STEP 0.08 under the face) for a same-piece box: hit = joint, no ink/bevel/edge wear. Rod long edges are joints. step_face(): faces >50% buried in a same-piece box (slice risers) get nothing, or sliced stocks get stripes. outer edge distance replaces Face.edge, so the materials' own edge wear follows it too.
- - style(): lit tops +6%, undersides -15%, near-black lifted to charcoal (LIFT 0.55 below lum 0.3), chips as clustered flecks just inside the outline (per-face quantile, CHIPS 5%+25%*wear, none on faces under 0.3 units), ink width ~1-1.6 texels, none on faces under 4 texels; ink is matt in _s. pbr.normal takes `cover` (no bevel at joints). paint(cache=) shares joint maps between skins.
- - kit.WEARS is one skin now, [('', LIKE_NEW 0.12, 0)]; by_wear() returns the plain model when there's one. The machinery stays: adding ('worn', 0.55, 0.4), ('battered', 1.0, 0.65) back makes textures item/weapon/{gun}[_{fin}][_{wear}], child models {base}/{skin}/... and a range_dispatch on minecraft:damage (RifleView.drawPiece would then need to copy DAMAGE/MAX_DAMAGE again; it was reverted). Never delete the melee *_worn textures: those are separate melee items.
- - Revolver (01:56 ask, reshaped 02:40): square butt with 45-degree cut corners (_end), stocks over the frame with a thicker core, guard polyline sweeping into the front strap, hammer spur under the sight line. sil.py side silhouettes in the weapons scratchpad are a fast check.

### zomboid-halo4-format.md (BACKGROUND)
- - Rot keys (3 x int16) are smallest-three: idx = (k2&1) + 2*(k1&1) of the dropped component (x,y,z,w order), k0&1 = dropped comp is negative; stored comps are the next three cyclically after idx, each = raw/32767/sqrt2. (janim.oq OQMODE='s3')

### zomboid-harvestman-den-tech.md (CORE)
- - Blocks: tools/city/harvestman_den.py (hd_ prefix, registered in photo_textures.py after market). hd_heap/_b/_tall/_low, hd_nest/_b, hd_debris/_b, hd_bones, hd_drag (axis), hd_gouges_0..2 (wall plate, facing = away from wall), hd_cable/_top/_end, hd_cocoon(+_top). Textures are procedural (no photos): `photo_textures.py ANY_DIR OUT hd_...` then copy textures/block/city + new pngs into a dir and run gen_blocks.py on it.
- - creature/HarvestmanDens: carve(level, lair, from) tears out non-full non-structural blocks within 4.5 (keeps stairs/doors/windows/rails/etc. by name), nest r<1.6, debris ring, heaps on wall-adjacent cells (never doorways/corridors/next to doors), gouges up the walls, drag trail toward `from`. bodies() = cocoon 60% + 3-6 kept corpses, first two 70% hung. setOut(level, body, at, from, hangChance): hang (needs ceiling 3-7 up, cable cells, neck at floor+2.05) / doorway (jambs + lintel) / against wall facing `from`. scrap()/work()/use(): heap shrinks one size per regrown leg.
- - Corpse: synced POSE (FALLEN 0, SAT 1, SLUMPED 2, HUNG 3, saved "pose"), keep(); HUNG skips falling. CorpseRenderer: posed ones use ZPosed.pose + entity yaw (not Deaths.yaw), hung sways/turns about ZPosed.NECK (1.68), culling box raised.

### zomboid-harvestman-game-tech.md (CORE)
- - HarvestmanLegs: SERVER steps the feet (never onto air: solid[] flags; hooks round edges); four VECTOR3 FOOT synced; client draws them 2 ticks behind (feetSeen) to match entity lerp.
- - HarvestmanEntity (Mob, noPhysics): 1200 hp, PLATES 0.1, CORE 2.0, LEG_HEALTH 250 each (lost bits, LEG_OFF), other damage x0.1. Dismember.shot skips it in the main loop and calls h.strike(); Guns.hitEntity returns early for it (no blood). Grab = player rides it (positionRider at GRIP, PlayerRidingMixin blocks sneak-dismount), struggle = jump presses (14+2*stage) or 120 damage taken; FEED_TICKS 200 drains via damage type zomboidcraft:harvestman. POSE HUNT/HOLD/FEED/DEAD, CAMO synced (mind.camo(): 0 feeding, ~0.15 striking, 1 roaming).

### zomboid-harvestman-goals-tech.md (CORE)
- - Cause: non-hunters rolled dice between WANDER/PERCH/CEILING/HIDE to random walls 16 blocks away. Mind.pick now sends non-hunters (target null) to goals.next(). The hunter's own WANDER/PERCH weights were removed.
- - SHELTER: by day when more than 64 from its den, it goes somewhere covered with no sky and waits till night.
- - HarvestmanChoice is pure Java and tested offline (scratchpad choice/T.java, 19 scenarios). Choices: STRIKE (in the den always; otherwise starving, alone, unaware and dark, never in grace or while hunting someone else), WARN (on its core ground within 28; remembers heeded/ignored), WATCH (OBSERVE from 18-24, slips away if looked at for 2 s), AVOID (HIDE), LET_PASS (while waiting or tending). It re-decides every 8 s per player.
- TEST ROOM (Josiah 00:15, playtest c4598728d): `/zc harvestman room` builds a closed deepslate hall 70 blocks above the player and teleports them in (HarvestmanTour). The hall is 31x22x7, with a partition and doorway at x20, a den beyond, pillars, a lit end and a ledge. A showcase Harvestman (mind.showcase: never strikes players, skips meet, never picked as hunter) tours 14 steps with captions: tend, patrol, lie_in_wait (doorway traffic seeded), zombie (2 zombies spawned), warn, observe, hide, shelter, stalk, ambush, feint, retreat, display, rage. `room next` skips a step; `room stop` discards  ...

### zomboid-harvestman-wave1-tech.md (SHARED)

### zomboid-harvestman-web-room.md (CORE)
- - The GLB is in cm (scaled 0.01), head +z, no root motion. Walk is 0.8 m/s native and run 3.03 m/s (from SpiderAnimator).
- Lesson (2026-10-30 11:18, fix in playtest fde3624): body vanished (legs only) whenever it stood still. SpiderIk's spin used AxisAngle4f on conj(last)*now; with no axis part and |q| a hair under 1 it returned a zero axis with angle ~7e-4, normalize() gave NaN and spin stayed NaN, killing every bone the spine/tail lead. Now axis/angle via atan2(|v|,w), skip |v|<1e-6, zero a non-finite spin. Never normalize an AxisAngle4f axis unchecked.
- OFF FOR FRAME RATE (Josiah 2026-11-01 06:59, "get rid of it for now"): config performance.harvestman (default false) gates it; director returns early, entities discard themselves, spawn command says it's off, save data kept (playtest 0ef0cb27e). Keep that gate in any later Harvestman work. When it returns: SpiderDraw.submit has no shadow-pass path (draws 18k tris + both glow passes in Photon's shadow pass); use ZDetail.shadowPass() to skip glow and draw a cheap version there.

### zomboid-hordes-never-vanish-rule.md (SHARED)
- Josiah 2026-11-04 06:07 (FPS thread): "we dont want a horde being encountered and having it disappear the next time a player returns."
- Fix: MobMixin.zomboidcraft$walkersStay makes removeWhenFarAway false for ZombieTypes.isWalker (playtest merge 6ed5d2db6). Do NOT use setPersistenceRequired for this: Population.fold() skips persistent zombies.
- **Why:** hordes are a gameplay thing he wants to stay real; folding into chunk records (Population.fold, countBeforeDelete 300 folds extras, never deletes) is the only allowed way to take them off the field.
- **How to apply:** any new walker removal path must fold to records, never discard. The real cap (autoCap 120-800) still limits how many come back live at once; the rest wait as records. Related: [[zomboid-population-tech]], [[zomboid-population-direction]].

### zomboid-house-alarms-tech.md (CORE)
- - **Placement:** world/county/House.alarm(height) runs after story(). Its dice are separate: SplittableRandom(interior.seed()*0x2545F4914F6CDD1D + 0xA1A2). It skips burnt houses (no interior), RUIN, TRAILER and CABIN.
- - Chance comes from House.setAlarmChance, which reads world.houseAlarms (sandbox "Alarm"). Values: never 0, extremely_rare .015, rare .04, sometimes .08 (default), often .15, very_often .25. These are MY GUESSES; check them against Zomboid's source on Josiah's PC.
- - The bell box goes at y=height-1 on the front wall, outside (z0-1, facing north). It tries offsets 2,-2,3,-3,1,-1,4,-4 from the door. If none fits, the house has no alarm.
- - An alarm needs power: PowerGrid.hasPower, or the battery. The battery lasts until offAt(district) + battery*2*world.alarmBatteryDays days; a value of -1 means no battery.
- - Cut payload: zomboidcraft:house_alarm_cut. It needs Electrical 5 and a screwdriver in either hand. The cut takes 120 ticks, or 60 at level 8 or above, and gives +15 XP. The cut works whether or not the alarm is ringing.

Others: weighted pick of POKER_NIGHT, HOUSE_PARTY, FOOTBALL_NIGHT, DEAD_DRUNK, GUNMAN_IN_BATHROOM, LOCKED_BATHROOM, BEDROOM_ZED, ZOMBIES_EATING, POLICE_AT_HOUSE, PRISON_ESCAPE, STUDENT_NIGHT (Zomboid's RBSafehouse + RDS* names). ### zomboid-injuries.md (SHARED)
- - Falls break legs (chance (dmg-3)/8 per leg). Zombies hit mostly the arms. Fire burns (15%). Blasts and arrows cut.
- - A bare broken leg means -25% speed and no sprinting; splinted it is -12%. Hurt arms lower attack damage and add rifle sway.
- - Test commands: `/zc injure <part> <scratch|cut|bite|burn|break>`, `/zc heal`, `/zc stats`.
- **Lesson (2026-10-25):** `@Inject(method="extractRenderState")` without a descriptor matched InventoryScreen's void render overload, and the game crashed at launch. CI's server smoke test never applies client mixins, so green CI does not prove a client mixin works. Always pin the full descriptor from probe output when a name may be overloaded.

### zomboid-kit-carry-rules.md (SHARED)
- - Weapons (guns and melee) never go in pockets (inv slots 9-35). Shotguns, rifles, snipers and melee can go on the back (gear SLING). Pistols, revolvers and magnums cannot sling.
- - A picked-up weapon goes to a free belt slot, then the sling, then bags (Carry.takeWeapon via an InventoryMixin add() hook). Shift-click quick move follows the same order and never uses pockets.
- - Bench: the gun stage is the main part of the screen (MID 314x196), and other panels sit around it. Right mouse only turns the gun and never selects. The footer says "RMB drag  Turn the gun".
- - To see the player's back in a client gametest, draw the render state on a test Screen with bodyRot 0 (KitUiTest BackView). Vanilla skips the LocalPlayer when the camera is another entity, and the mod's F5 cam is too close.
- - Melee sling confirmed on CI 2026-10-27: the axe lies diagonally, head over the right shoulder. Test gotcha: a test Screen must override isPauseScreen() false, or the integrated server stops ticking and the WORN sync never runs.

### zomboid-mesh-guns-tech.md (SHARED)
- - tools/meshguns/pack.py SPEC.json -> assets/zomboidcraft/meshes/<id>.zmesh (v3; header carries outline width). Toon-painted textures stacked into one atlas, named parts (body + pieces cut by z/y/x boxes: handle, mag, slide, optic = cut away unused). Fit modes: `fit: <gun>` (muzzle on our muzzle, bore on hip/muzzle_flash, scaled to our length, or `length` px, or real size `unit_mm`), `box: <model>` (melee, bolts: longest side + centre), `axes` [x,y,z source axes] instead of forward/up, `shift`. Scrambled like cars (CarAssets.unscramble).
- - tools/meshguns/skins.json: gun -> mesh id or {mesh, keep:[block parts kept], parts:{block part: mesh id}, models:{item/weapon path: mesh id}}. skin.py rewrites items/<gun>.json + <gun>_loose_*.json (body/charging_handle->handle/slide/mag/stock; *_open gets an offset; irons dropped unless kept; melee model + _worn -> whole mesh). Already-special nodes are not re-swapped, so regen from weapons.py (kit.mesh_skin runs per gun/melee and again for every skins.json entry at the end).
- - Swapped so far: assault_rifle, smg, pistol (keeps block mag), magnum (cylinder offset), shotgun (pump=handle), hunting_rifle (keeps block mag, scope cut), crossbow (+bolt mesh pbr_crossbow_bolt, ammo pbr_bolt), machete. Specs live in scratch packs/pbr_*.json (not repo; regenerate with prep.py side views).
- - 2026-10-30 (perf thread): run `python3 tools/meshguns/lod.py` after any pack.py run. It cuts meshes over 6000 triangles to 1/3 with meshoptimizer (seams kept, Josiah approved the look), and tools/meshguns/lod_done.json records what has been cut.

### zomboid-nightvision-tech.md (SHARED)
- Items (gear/ package): combat_helmet and nvg_head_mount are HeadGearItem with Equippable HEAD (no asset, so vanilla's head layer uses the item model). nvg_gen1/gen3/white are NightVisionItem (Generation enum, key = item id). aa_battery is BatteryItem. Models and lang come from `python3 tools/weapons/headgear.py`.
- Components: `zomboidcraft:goggles` = Headgear.Mounted(goggles stack, up) on the headgear, only when mounted. `zomboidcraft:battery` = seconds left on the goggles; absent means never picked up, and the server tick (Headgear.settleFound) gives found goggles 20-80% (full in creative). Change headgear only through Headgear.mount/unmount/setUp so CUSTOM_MODEL_DATA strings [goggle key, "up"/"down"] stay right.
- Not done yet: gear/attachment tier rolls (no Rarity on gear yet; Rolls.roll would roll anything with a Rarity). Untested in game, including Photon/Iris with the post effect.

### zomboid-open-plan-rule.md (SHARED)
- **Why:** ring corridors with offices round the windows and rooms wrapped round the core read as cramped mazes, and seating facing a wall makes no sense.
- **How to apply:** for public and work floors, use open plan: one big floor out to the glass, only a few glass-walled rooms, and only the essential rooms by the core. Bedrooms, flats, dorms, care-home rooms and separate firms keep walls. Never point a sofa or chair at a wall close up.
- - `Floorplan.cut(..., cap)` stops a strip early.
- - `Floorplan.place()` turns a seat round, or skips it, if a WALL, CORE or DOOR is 1-2 cells ahead.

### zomboid-opening-guns-tech.md (SHARED)
- - Revolver cylinder (RifleView): cylAngle always a whole number of chambers at rest (16-stave shape never jumps); each shot cylAngle -= chamberStep; loading turns chamber `shells` to LOAD_SPOT (-60) and settles to the nearest chamber on stop. Its rounds come up in SCREEN space from lower left (cylRound, CYL_HAND offset), not the gun frame: after the crane swing gun-frame "down" points sideways and the hand box covered everything. Shooting forearm drops to RIGHT_LOAD while open. Blocky fists (0.15 blocks) dwarf a revolver cylinder (0.09): keep the fist under the round, never over the cylinder.
- - Hammer (2026-10-27, Josiah): revolver.py/pistol.py hammer_piece is the kit's `slide` (the pistol's own slide is its `handle`); rig hammer (pin, item px) + hammerCock. REVOLVER/MAGNUM (his 08:27 ask): rests DOWN, pin at the hammer's foot (y 0.0, 22 deg) so only the top swings; each shot RifleView.hammerAmount snaps it back 0.03 s and drops it 0.025 s, inside the 80 ms flash. A higher pin or bigger angle drops the spur into the frame/grip. PISTOL: cocked while loaded, falls with the shot, slide recocks (rig.cycles). Server aim-raise cock click removed. weapons.py regen rewrites lang/en_us.json ...
- - Ground/item-frame display of parts (Josiah 10-27: crossbow bolts lay 4 blocks long, then 'all attachments expanded when dropped'): small_display(real=...) draws attachments, mags and bolts at TRUE size, 16/39.37/IN = 0.496 (builds are in inches); model.fit has depth/scale args. Ammo boxes still fit-to-size.

### zomboid-population-tech.md (SHARED)
- - `Population.java`: chunk attachment `zomboidcraft:population` = Pop(int[] recs, given, day). Chunks within 8 of a player are queued and filled (2.5 ms per tick). Every 10 ticks: street records wake within streetRange 96 (|dy| <= 40), indoor ones within indoorRange 32 and indoorFloors 9 (or 8 blocks and 24 up/down), nearest first, perStep 24, under realCap 250. Noise.emit calls Population.wake (radius >= 20) so records in earshot come out and head to the noise. Idle walkers (no target, nothing heard, no lost limbs, 200+ ticks old) past range + foldMargin 16 fold back into their chunk's record ...
- - Doors/windows (BashGoal): blows counted per block (door lower half) in a shared map, forgotten after 5 minutes untouched; `zombies.doorHits` 120 (was a 1-in-12 roll), `windowHits` 45; windows = GLASS sound, no light.
- - KEEP (UI thread, 2026-10-27 22:57): spawn picker calls Population.keepClear(level, pos) -> level attachment zomboidcraft:clear_zones (<=256); Spots.scan seeding skips spots within 14 sideways, -4..+10 vertically; drops records and live zombies there. Preserve this skip in any seeding/record rework.
- - 2026-10-28 stall fix (branch ma5ppf): Census used the world's CountyGen and its 10-place PlaceCache, evicting places near players (rebuilt under the global BUILD_LOCK while chunks waited: lag with FPS fine). Census now runs its own CountyGen (Census.side) with PlaceCache(4, borrow = world cache): reuses world-built places, never evicts them, standBack() waits up to 15 s while world-gen builds want the lock. Keep this if Census is reworked.

### zomboid-quickslots-tech.md (CORE)
- - 0 HANDS, 1-2 HIP, 3 HOLSTER, 4 SHEATH, 5 BACK (the old sling), 6-7 POUCH, 8 SPARE (never open).
- - `Carry.kit()` runs every 10 ticks and skips spectators.
- - It spills anything that no longer fits its quick slot through `inventory.add`, or drops it.
- - A client-only `setSelectedSlot` makes scrolling skip closed slots. It treats a move to an adjacent slot as a scroll; a number key for a closed slot does nothing.

### zomboid-rail-lights-tech.md (SHARED)

- - In-game check: branch `gunlook` (test only, never merge): CI client gametest, shots to branch gunlook-shots.

### zomboid-reach-shader-tech.md (BACKGROUND)
- - 26.3 API facts (probed): DynamicGpuData.writeTransform(mv, textureMat) and 4-arg (mv, colour, offset, textureMat); RenderSystem.getSamplerCache().getClampToEdge/getRepeat(FilterMode, mips); CommandEncoder.writeToTexture(tex, NativeImage, mip, ...); custom pipelines compile on demand via RenderSystem.getCompiledPipeline, no registry.
- - EXPORTER BUG FIXED 2026-11-09 14:50 (cloud lexport2.py): cluster (terrain) lightmap-uv buffers only cover the lightmapped parts (first N vertices; water/decal parts with vertex type 138 come after), so the old `len(LUV)>=cnt` test dropped TEXCOORD_1 from ALL terrain: every mission's ground was lit by a fake sun. Now per part `vu.max()<len(LUV)`. ALL glbs on the PC predate this: the PC re-export must use the fixed lexport2 (backup tools to project files before the PC session).
- - Material gotchas (2026-11-09): shader group strings can carry a trailing space ('rmw ' water) so trim before comparing; water has no textures (needs its own look: deep colour water_color_pure + fresnel to fog/sky); additive sky layers must fade with fog (c*=1-f), not mix to fog colour, or skies blow out white; terrain global_albedo_tint scales layers; New Alexandria needs height haze under the towers (preview hfb=350 hft=120 hfd=6, metres).
- - 2026-11-09 evening (playtest 5a5012698, CI green): BLEND+ADDITIVE+MULTIPLY now drawn together farthest-first (ReachMeshes.drawHaloSeeThrough) - fixed m45 orbit horizon band cutting through the planet and m10 sky panels; decals: alpha_map = plain slot 5 (flag 128, alpha=min(r,a)), multiply decals flag 256 own pipeline (DST_COLOR,ZERO). lmat KEEP now has alpha_map (PC re-export must use it). Water (rmw) is still NOT in the in-game shader (no base_map -> plain path); preview viewer draws it blended: scattered wcol*L, sky sheen by fresnel, alpha 0.45+0.55F, sine ripples.
- - Lone Wolf (m70_bonus): no squads; cams from real/sq_m70_bonus.json (flat cells of the glb). Epilogue sbsp has no Lbsp (no lightmap) so it is skipped.
- - 2026-11-10 07:43 FIRST IN-GAME RUN on world2 garbled (exploded triangles, even with Iris pack turned off mid-game): level buffers use RenderTypes.entityCutout().format(), which with Iris+pack is Iris's longer entity vertex, but ReachShader pipelines were bound to DefaultVertexFormat.ENTITY. Fixed (playtest 7e6b08cb7): ReachShader.forFormat(kind, u.format) per-layout pipelines; plain surfaces of a level uploaded in another layout are skipped (restart after toggling packs). reach.json "shader": false also turns the shader off. Fallback config (old world\ glbs + towers) at PC additions\halo-rea ...

### zomboid-reload-rules.md (SHARED)
- - Handgun grips: no finger collision during the reload (his static poses clip slightly; never change those, fix only inside the reload).
- - First person shows the whole arm (upper arm too), never cut at the elbow.
- - Guns with a cocking handle on the RIGHT (L86 type): left hand does the mag from the left hip, then plants back on its grip and the RIGHT hand pulls the handle (no left-arm crossover).
- - He judged the natural whole-arm solver "100% better"; joints must move together, no unnatural bends or collisions.
- - 03:22: Honey Badger type (charging handle on top/back): the right hand works it after the left hand has finished the mag. Fingers must never twitch.
- - 03:24: as soon as a hand finishes its loading job and goes back to the gun, it must sit EXACTLY in his set grip (position and fingers) while the other hand cocks; no grip drift before the final frame.
- - 05:42 (10 notes): slaps never bend a finger backwards; handgun thumb stays exactly in its static grip spot all reload; third-person STANDING pistol/revolver reloads at chest height (not waist); always aim the loading face at the loading hand (L86 bullpup: trigger hand holds gun out + tilts mag to the left hand so the arm doesn't cross the body; 870: right arm tilts and pushes the gun forward for the left hand); revolvers in FP keep the gun's static direction, swing cylinder out, nose up to dump, level to load, speedloader taken away; Big Boar: left hand lays the gun over (bolt/chamber face u ...
- - Editor v13 (06:2x): hand keys carry fk = {cl:[shrug,fwd] (TP only, FP rig has no clavicle), sh:[raise,fwd,twist], el, tw, wr:[bend,side], f:{th:[curl,spread,roll], i/m/r/p:[curl,spread]}} in degrees, applied by engine.js Rig.userPose after the solve (forward kinematics, the hand moves with the joint). Bake must honour fk when reloads go in-game. S&W 500 v6: R REST all reload, L does cylinder, speedloader from POUCH_L, puts it away, closes.
- - 06:25-06:35: every hand key time gets a key on the other hand: {k:'mid'} keys (engine handTrack/handAt) keep the motion exact and only ADD his dpx/r/curl/flat/fk on top, fading to the neighbouring keys. Views sticky. Automatic finger unclip OFF for all reloads (spec.autoFingers to re-enable): it made fingers twitch whenever he moved a hand; fingers stay exactly as posed. Editor v14.

### zomboid-road-stories-tech.md (CORE)
- - **world/county/RoadStories.java** (public): Zomboid's 13 RVS stories. Every highway and county road is cut into 256-block stretches. Each stretch rolls from hash(road.seed ^ 0x5701, w, 313) with chance BASE 0.3 x rate. Rates: never 0, extremely_rare 0.2, rare 0.5, sometimes 1, often 2, very_often 3, always 1000.
- - **Fits:** the road is level (no asphalt half steps), not on a bridge, at least 12 blocks clear of a jam (Jams.jammed), clear of places, junctions and the railway, and 48 or more blocks from the road's ends. About 32% of stretches fit. Seed 12345 has 329 km of road, so "always" gives about 408 stories and "rare" about 60.
- - **VEHICLES:** poses "@wrecked^roof" and "^side" are used for the flipped crash and the ambulance. There is no hitched trailer, so the trailer crash uses two cars (pickup + horse_trailer). mesh.py previews the poses.

### zomboid-rolls-tech.md (CORE)
- - Where tier comes from: Fabric `LootTableEvents.MODIFY_DROPS`; place from the loot table path (home_guns/basement_guns = gun_cabinet, guns/pawn = gun_shop, car/police = police, car/swat = military, entities/* = dead, etc). Dense horde (20+ zombies within 48) = 50% chance of +1 tier. Items never from a loot table roll "unknown" 1-3 in the Carry tick; CaseItem rolls "case". Never distance-based.
- - GunFinish.line returns Component.empty() (never null) since 23:20 (branch finish-fix, playtest 81923c5): the kit screen called getString() on it.
- - WORLD LOOT thread (2026-10-27): owns non-weapon items. WEAPONS keeps gun-adjacent consumables (cleaning kits, oil, parts, reloading supplies, holsters, slings, range bags, ammo boxes) and gives that thread the ids to place. It may add a tag-based fallback to Rarity.of and an ItemCard on WeaponTip/KitDraw, and may use kit.paint/toon.py (no behaviour changes).

### zomboid-town-station-tech.md (CORE)
- - Hand-off: Railway.stations() picks each RAIL stop with street>0 and mode GROUND, steps street+24 off the platform side, keys the PITCH cell; markTiles copies them into TileRails.stations. DowntownSite.railRoles gives that block Role.STATION when its street side faces the line; station(b) centres a 28x17 lot on the stop middle (falls back to walkups if the block is too small), fills the rest of the block with normal frontage and marks the canopy cells BUILT so no street lamps go under it.
- - Checks run: orientcheck 0 problems; walkcheck no blocked doors (only furniture tops unreachable).

### zomboid-trains-direction.md (SHARED)
- - **Driverless:** each train loops its own route, run by a robot conductor. It is our own character (working name OTTO), inspired by the Black Ops 2 TranZit bus driver. Use no franchise names.
- - The train skips other stops to come to you, unless someone nearer on the line pressed first.
- - **Downtown:** a New York style island city, cut off by wide channels and reached by big bridges. One bridge carries the railway. He said to make it "as massive as you need".

### zomboid-trains-tech.md (CORE)
- - Calls: block zomboidcraft:train_call_button[facing], G (interact) sends Call; nearest stop within 48. Flare above ground / siren in subway (Beacons) make Noise. Train skips to the nearest call ahead; doors 30 s (config trains.doorSeconds).
- - OTTO = robot conductor (Otto.java), voiced lines; no franchise names.
- - 18:50 2026-10-27: stop buttons (TrainCar.STOP_BUTTONS, StopRequest packet, TrainRun.requestStop/halt, doorSide 0); speeds 10/9 (defaults round 9); no sleepers on rw_street_bed. County lines arrive via lineExact.

"Grand hunt and tactical survival building bases rather than desperate survival measures." Doc reworked: needs = slow upkeep, near panic-proof; marine base skills + one specialist role, no negative traits (2 positive picks); base building a main pillar (room functions, fortification set, outposts W26); purge = grand hunt (hunt board, HVTs).

### zomboid-world-tech.md (CORE)
- - Offline previews: this env can't reach npm or CDNs (no three.js). Use tools/county/CountyPreview.java (dump) -> tools/county/vanilla.py (vanilla block stand-ins) -> tools/city/mesh.py --extra -> tools/county/view.html (plain WebGL2) via tools/county/shot.js and a local http server. City textures: copy src/main/resources/assets/zomboidcraft/textures/block/city into a work dir; regenerate _nrm_gl maps from _n for mesh.py.
- - Site builders (world/county/*Site, Kit, Camp, Bldg, Placer): Kit's use[] grid (FREE/ROAD/WALK/VERGE/LOT/PARK/BUILT) decides what overgrows; claim BUILT for yards. Voxels.get returns null for explicit "air", so lawn before builders, never after (it refills pools). Mod AXIS blocks are horizontal only (tire[axis=y] is invalid). Never pass "air" as a Bldg.door type (makes "air[facing...]"); set air directly. Placer.vehicle(x,y,z,yaw,"a,b",seed) spawns a car; each one costs FPS, keep a site near 30.

### zomboid-zbody-tech.md (SHARED)
- **Java** (src/client/.../zombie/body/): ZBodyMesh loads .bin ("ZBD1"; quad flags SMALL=1 STUMP=2 CUT=4 BODY=8). ZMove = hand port of move.js, 11 blendable modes (IDLE SHAMBLE ALERT CHASE ATTACK DOWN CRAWL_REST/SLOW/FAST/ALERT/GRAB); keep it in step with move.js. ZBodyAnimator picks the mode per zombie (attack = rising getAttackAnim, aggressive+moving = chase, |head yaw|>10° = alert, swimming pose = down/crawl) and blends at 6/s; gait integrates walkAnimationPos. ZBodyLayer builds bone matrices (JOML rotateXYZ = three XYZ; hips rotateYXZ standing) and emits via submitCustomGeometry; preview→mod ...
- **WalkerRenderer:** getRenderType returns null when the new body is on (old model hidden), vanilla layers wrapped in OldBodyOnly, old lie tilt skipped. look.newZombies=false restores the old model.
- **Follow-through (2026-10-31, Blender tutorial thread, playtest 73150599a):** ZBodyAnimator.flow() adds spring bow/lean/head-lead/arm swing from speed accel, body-yaw turn rate and landings, standing only (skipped crawling, lying, breaking in, dead). Added AFTER move.pose/hop/events and BEFORE ZHit shot jerks. Signs per ZMove doc: spine x>0 forward, spine z>0 to its right, head y<0 turns right, shoulder x<0 forward.

### zomboid-zombie-spawning-tech.md (SHARED)
- - Vanilla night spawns (NATURAL). MobFilter only removes non-walker monsters, so vanilla zombie/husk/zombie villager still spawn. They are held to `perched` by `mixin/PathfinderMobMixin` (checkSpawnRules HEAD). Not MobMixin: PathfinderMob overrides Mob.checkSpawnRules without calling super, so a Mob hook never fires for zombies.

### zomboid-zone-stories-tech.md (CORE)
- - **world/county/ZoneStories.java** (public): 11 stories in 4 zones. FOREST (t.forest > 0.6): forest camp, forest camp eaten, hunter, trapper, burying. SHORE (lake or river within 9 straight along x/z): fishing trip. BEACH (that water is the sea): beach party. FIELD (forest < 0.3, half the field rolls skipped): BBQ, music fest, baseball, field burial. No zone near town (city > 0.15 or sprawl > 0.35).
- - **Fit:** no place within radius + SKIRT, no hideout, roads more than half + 9 + radius away, rail more than radius + 30 away, ground max - min <= 1. Water is allowed only in front, and only for the water stories, which face it (local -z).
- - **Hooks:** emitted through CountyGen.stories with the same Out as the road stories. vegetation() skips trees within radius + 3 (ZoneStories.inside on a per-chunk near() list); camp ground gets short grass and leaf litter only. StoryMarks.register sets ZoneStories.setRate from Sandbox "ZoneStoryChance".
